2026-10-30T10:23:19.1594497Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve the round-7 PP4 bundle (memory-bound Prefill kernels, MegaMoE shared reciprocal, gated FA3 pipe row) with a fresh image

One image build and one PP4 serving run at concurrency 232 on top of the
9832.74 configuration (41005872470; round 6, 41020974704, repeated it at
9828.95 because all three of its candidates lost their gates). Every kernel
that has never served passes an in-job endurance gate before the workers
start and falls back to the pinned one otherwise. No profile pass is
requested in this message.

Why these candidates. The nsys pass of 40964714831, split per stage, shows
the PP0 period is almost all kernel work: stages 0-2 idle only 0.40-0.48 ms
per microbatch. Per 24-layer stage and microbatch: MegaMoE 64.5 ms, FA3 20.8,
QKV 12.3, O 10.7, the one-pass MoE input 2.87 (119 us per layer against an
HBM floor of about 55 us), the FP32 Q/K norm writer 2.02 (84 us per layer,
floor about 58 us). 10,000 needs about 2 ms less work per period.

The image must be rebuilt: the DeepGEMM host series gained
sm90-prefill-shared-rcp and lost split-ldsm and combine-norm (11 patches
apply in order to a clean 1c8138a). The SGLang overlays lost combine-norm
and gained prefill-qknorm-fp32-heads and prefill-moe-input-onepass-split
(31 overlays apply in order to the image source).

Candidates, each against the current pin, with the expected saving per
period if adopted:
1. Q/K norm fp32-heads4/8 (972e703): one CTA covers 4 or 8 heads and loads
   the position and cos/sin row once instead of once per head. Every output
   byte must equal the fp32 kernel. Expected 15-22 us per layer, 0.36-0.53 ms.
2. MoE input onepass-split (972e703, patch regenerated without combine-norm
   in d718000): the 32 column groups of each 64-row block run in two
   programs, two programs per SM, and a small kernel finishes the norm and
   top-8. Residual, FP8 codes and pad slots bit-equal; scales and routing
   within the FP32 partial-sum order bounds. Expected 30-45 us per layer of
   mostly idle time, 0.3-0.6 ms after the power-cap factor.
3. MegaMoE four-stage-lean-split-rcp (911a2e8): SwiGLU shares one MUFU
   reciprocal between two columns (MUFU 130 to 98 per thread in the FC1
   epilogue), with a warp-wide fallback for overflow. Tolerance gate plus a
   new untimed wide routing that drives the fallback. Expected 7-15 us per
   layer, 0.17-0.36 ms.
4. The hardened pipelined-tile Prefill FA3 row (d8ba09f, gate 50ab866):
   screened against the pin and, if it wins, 1200 eager serving-path calls
   per GPU with copy and GEMM traffic around them before adoption. Measured
   -3% of FA3 on H100, about 0.6 ms.

Removed from the job: the combine-norm gate and the Decode FA3 verify
screen (it timed out at 175 s in 41020974704 and cost about 160 s).
SGLANG_LOG_MS=1 for PP4 workers to time the start-burst stall between 0.8
and 1.5 s.

If every candidate is adopted the period falls by about 1.4-2.1 ms
(1.2-1.8%), about 9,950-10,010. Selection steps add about 0.5-1.3 min before
worker start compared with 41005872470 (Slurm 12:39).

Validation: full pytest suite, bash -n, py_compile of the patched SGLang
files, overlay chain applied on the image source; worker branches report
sm_90a SASS and Triton compiles without spills.
